> * * *
> 
> ## 4.1. Graphene
> 
> ## 4.1. 石墨烯
> 
> Graphene, a layer-dependent 2D material, showcases unique interlayer non-equivalent stacking characteristics derived from its highly symmetrical honeycomb lattice structure, which earlier classified it as a non-ferroelectric material. 石墨烯是一种层数依赖的二维材料，展示了源自其高度对称蜂窝晶格结构的独特层间非等效堆垛特性，这使其早期被归类为非铁电材料。
> 
> In 2017, Li and Wu found that the bilayer or multilayer stacking of materials could manifest specific periodic ferroelectric domain patterns, inducing OOP polarization and exhibiting sliding ferroelectric behavior [17]. 2017年，Li和Wu发现，材料的双层或多层堆垛可以表现出特定的周期性铁电畴图案，诱导面外极化并表现出滑动铁电行为 [17]。
> 
> Significant ferroelectric hysteresis was observed in a bilayer twisted superlattice of graphene, in a device constituted by a 30° twist angle in the upper layer of h-BN and graphene and a 0° twist angle in the lower layer (Fig. 9a) [139]. 在石墨烯的双层扭转超晶格中观察到了显著的铁电滞后现象，该器件由上层h-BN和石墨烯的30°扭转角以及下层0°扭转角构成（图9a） [139]。
> 
> Notably, graphene retains centrosymmetric in both monolayer and bilayer forms, lacking ferroelectric properties. 值得注意的是，石墨烯在单层和双层形式下均保持中心对称，缺乏铁电特性。
> 
> However, Yang et al. revealed that the ferroelectric properties of graphene could be achieved when the number of layers exceeds three via DFT calculations [121]. 然而，Yang等人通过DFT计算揭示，当层数超过三层时，石墨烯可以获得铁电特性 [121]。
> 
> Particularly, OOP polarization was not observed in trilayer graphene structures with mirror symmetry and inversion symmetry. 特别是，在具有镜面对称和反演对称的三层石墨烯结构中未观察到面外极化。
> 
> In the exploration of the Moiré superlattice of graphene, for the study of the stacking order of ABABAB and BCBACA types, the introduction of interlayer relaxation generates domains of Moiré periods [140]. 在探索石墨烯的莫尔超晶格时，对于ABABAB和BCBACA类型堆垛顺序的研究，引入层间弛豫会产生莫尔周期的畴 [140]。
> 
> As the Moiré period increases, the relaxation increases, which corresponds to the change in the energy of the structure. 随着莫尔周期的增加，弛豫增加，这对应于结构能量的变化。
> 
> Once the relaxation accumulated to a certain level, the energy was able to overcome the corresponding sliding barriers and interlayer sliding occurred, advancing from the ABABAB stacks to the BCBACA stacks. 一旦弛豫积累到一定程度，能量就能够克服相应的滑动势垒，发生层间滑动，从ABABAB堆垛进入BCBACA堆垛。
> 
> This non-local relaxation was one of the reasons for interlayer sliding. 这种非局域弛豫是层间滑动的原因之一。
> 
> When it comes to four-layer graphene, sliding across the layers was possible due to specific stacking configurations of the interlayer structure, which broke the central symmetry resulting in the generation of ferroelectricity, and in the theoretical prediction for the four-layer structure, the interlayer sliding carries out a modulation of polarization, with an accumulation of polarizations caused by multilayer sliding [121]. 当涉及四层石墨烯时，由于层间结构的特定堆垛构型，跨层滑动成为可能，这破坏了中心对称性，导致铁电性的产生，在对四层结构的理论预测中，层间滑动对极化进行调制，多层滑动导致极化积累 [121]。
> 
> Additionally, superlattices could be created at the level of twists in the study of multilayer graphene. 此外，在多層石墨烯研究中，超晶格可以在扭转层面产生。
> 
> Ruiz et al. established twist angles in four-layer graphene, reconstructing the lattice through twisting and achieving polarization switching via sliding, which indicated that cross-layer sliding and twist angle construction were effective strategies for inducing sliding ferroelectricity in multilayer graphene [141]. Ruiz等人在四层石墨烯中建立了扭转角，通过扭转重建晶格并通过滑动实现极化翻转，这表明跨层滑动和扭转角构建是诱导多层石墨烯滑动铁电性的有效策略 [141]。
> 
> For the calculation of multilayer sliding paths, Fig. 9b and c shows the paths for which there was a corresponding energy conversion in sliding across layers [26,139]. 对于多层滑动路径的计算，图9b和c显示了跨层滑动中存在相应能量转换的路径 [26,139]。
> 
> The presence of Moiré stripes in the twist of graphene could affect the electron transport in the interlayer. 石墨烯扭转中莫尔条纹的存在会影响层间电子传输。
> 
> Especially, interlayer sliding could effectively induce the generation of polarization, and change the position of the atoms in the sliding layers, complex stacking patterns and interlayer distances inhibit interlayer charge transfer, superlattices have new Brillouin zones and band gaps near the Dirac stack, and the energy bands undergo flattening, which affects charge transfer and thus polarization. 特别是，层间滑动可以有效诱导极化的产生，改变滑动层中原子位置，复杂的堆垛模式和层间距离抑制层间电荷转移，超晶格在狄拉克堆垛附近具有新的布里渊区和带隙，能带发生扁平化，这会影响电荷转移从而影响极化。
> 
> **Brillouin zone**: 布里渊区，固体物理学中描述晶体中电子波矢空间的倒易晶格原胞，用于表征能带结构。 **Dirac cone**: 狄拉克锥，石墨烯等材料中电子能量与动量呈线性关系的能带结构，交点处形成锥形。
> 
> The investigation of graphene mainly focuses on the number of stacked layers and the form of stacking, the way of electron transfer, the interlayer sliding barriers, as well as the movement and formation of domain walls [140,142]. 对石墨烯的研究主要集中在堆垛层数和堆垛形式、电子转移方式、层间滑动势垒以及畴壁的运动和形成 [140,142]。
> 
> The number of layers of graphene was correlated with the form of sliding, with the appearance of stacked forms and torsion angles forming the corresponding superlattices, and interlayer twisting and sliding being effective for polarization modulation. 石墨烯的层数与滑动形式相关，堆垛形式和扭转角的出现形成了相应的超晶格，层间扭转和滑动对极化调制有效。
> 
> The existence of spontaneous valley polarization in bilayer, trilayer, and twisted graphene, as well as the butterfly hysteresis phenomena was observed in five-layer graphene due to spontaneous valley polarization, suggesting potential applications of graphene’s multiferroic properties in devices [143]. 在双层、三层和扭转石墨烯中存在自发谷极化，并且在五层石墨烯中由于自发谷极化观察到了蝴蝶形滞后现象，这表明石墨烯的多铁特性在器件中具有潜在应用 [143]。
> 
> **Valley polarization**: 谷极化，指在动量空间中，不同能谷（如K和K'谷）的电子占据数不平衡的状态。
> 
> The long period of the superlattice, where the superlattice was formed at one interface and crystals at the other, creates a larger ferroelectric polarization, where ferroelectric switching could be applied to topological valley currents and other properties [139]. 超晶格的长周期，即在一边界面形成超晶格而另一边是晶体，会产生更大的铁电极化，其中铁电翻转可应用于拓扑谷电流和其他特性 [139]。
> 
> This may subsequently influence the transfer of electrons under an applied electric field. 这可能随后影响在外加电场下的电子转移。
> 
> To investigate this phenomenon of angles and orientations, Zheng et al. focused on Moiré superlattices with h-BN and graphene and examined the ripple superlattice potential between two h-BN layers with bilayer graphene stacked in a Bernal arrangement, achieving switchable ferroelectricity under an external electric field with significant hysteresis, as shown in Fig. 9d and e [26,139]. 为了研究这种角度和取向现象，Zheng等人专注于h-BN和石墨烯的莫尔超晶格，研究了以Bernal堆垛方式堆垛双层石墨烯的两层h-BN之间的波纹超晶格势，在外加电场下实现了可翻转的铁电性，并具有显著的滞后现象，如图9d和e所示 [26,139]。
> 
> This system exhibited a novel type of Moiré ferroelectricity, with changes in alignment between the top and bottom h-BN layers creating twist angles between 0° and 60°, resulting in a strong response to external electric fields. 该体系表现出一种新型的莫尔铁电性，顶部和底部h-BN层之间的取向变化产生0°到60°之间的扭转角，导致对外加电场的强烈响应。
> 
> The asymmetry between layers manifests as an asymmetry between the valence and conduction bands [26]. 层间的不对称性表现为价带和导带之间的不对称性 [26]。
>

>

(c) The lattice structure of Bernal-stacked bilayer graphene [26]. Copyright 2020, Springer Nature. (d) Double-layer graphene device, graphene is twisted to form a superlattice [139]. Copyright 2022, Springer Nature. (e) Resistance property of graphene device [26]. Copyright 2020, Springer Nature. 图9. (a) 由石墨烯和h-BN形成的扭转超晶格范德华器件示意图。(b) 转移曲线的滞后性能。[139] 版权2022，施普林格自然。(c) Bernal堆垛双层石墨烯的晶格结构 [26]。版权2020，施普林格自然。(d) 双层石墨烯器件，石墨烯扭转形成超晶格 [139]。版权2022，施普林格自然。(e) 石墨烯器件的电阻特性 [26]。版权2020，施普林格自然。
> 
> * * *
> 
> ## 4.2. BN
> 
> ## 4.2. 氮化硼
> 
> The structural features and underlying mechanisms of sliding of the BN including h-BN and r-BN showed significant differences depending on the preparation methods employed. BN（包括h-BN和r-BN）的滑动结构特征和潜在机制因所采用的制备方法而异，显示出显著差异。
> 
> The work of BN has been on the investigation and application of torsion-promoted superlattice formation and intercalation in BN to achieve cross-layer sliding, as well as the motions of the domain walls of the spontaneous polarization region during sliding. BN的研究工作集中在扭转促进的超晶格形成和插层以实现跨层滑动的研究和应用，以及滑动过程中自发极化区域畴壁的运动。
> 
> Extensive investigations have been carried out on the sliding ferroelectricity of h-BN, focusing on the effects of stacking shapes, twist angles, and substrates on the sliding ferroelectricity. 对h-BN的滑动铁电性进行了广泛研究，重点是堆垛形状、扭转角和衬底对滑动铁电性的影响。
> 
> Yasuda et al. exhibited ferroelectric properties absent in monolayer h-BN, but between bilayer structures, interfacial ferroelectricity could appear. Yasuda等人展示了单层h-BN中不存在的铁电特性，但在双层结构之间，界面铁电性可能出现。
> 
> This was due to the rotation and stacking of two h-BN layers, with the B atom of one layer above the N atom of the other, leading to overlapped h-BN atomic orbitals and electronic orbital distortion of N, generating electric dipole moments. 这是由于两层h-BN的旋转和堆垛，其中一层的B原子位于另一层的N原子上方，导致h-BN原子轨道重叠和N的电子轨道畸变，产生电偶极矩。
> 
> Altering the stacking order of the N atom of one layer relative to the B atom of the other reverses the OOP ferroelectric polarization. 改变一层N原子相对于另一层B原子的堆垛顺序可以反转面外铁电极化。
> 
> This phenomenon could be observed by PFM, demonstrating the existence of OOP ferroelectric polarization. 这种现象可以通过PFM观察到，证明了面外铁电极化的存在。
> 
> Twisted h-BN with a 0.6° twist broke the original symmetry than untwisted h-BN, indicating that polarization in twisted h-BN occurs gradually, with minimal pinning effects during switching, facilitating domain wall movement. 与未扭转的h-BN相比，扭转0.6°的h-BN打破了原始对称性，表明扭转h-BN中的极化是逐渐发生的，翻转过程中的钉扎效应最小，有利于畴壁运动。
> 
> Sliding occurs between two h-BN layers, and breaking symmetry through polarization switching could be integrated into devices [144]. 滑动发生在两层h-BN之间，通过极化翻转打破对称性可以集成到器件中 [144]。
> 
> Explorations into the temperature dependence of ferroelectric behavior found that strong covalent bonds within h-BN could suppress IP thermal vibrations of atoms, rendering polarization insensitive to temperature and ensuring stability. 对铁电行为温度依赖性的探索发现，h-BN内强共价键可以抑制原子的面内热振动，使极化对温度不敏感并确保稳定性。
> 
> Zhao et al. showed that a 6 % OOP compressive strain enhances anharmonic scattering using DFT principles, reducing thermal conductivity, whereas an 18 % strain increases anharmonic scattering, raising thermal conductivity. Zhao等人利用DFT原理表明，6%的面外压缩应变会增强非谐散射，降低热导率，而18%的应变会增加非谐散射，提高热导率。
> 
> This comprehensive analysis of the impact of sliding between bilayer h-BN on thermal conductivity elucidates the effect of sliding ferroelectricity on changes in interlayer strain and constraints on ferroelectricity. 这种对双层h-BN之间滑动对热导率影响的综合分析，阐明了滑动铁电性对层间应变变化和铁电性约束的影响。
> 
> In bilayer stacking, B-N stacking was more conducive to device cooling compared to B-B stacking, which has a smaller bandgap and higher electrical conductivity. 在双层堆垛中，与B-B堆垛相比，B-N堆垛更有利于器件冷却，B-B堆垛具有较小的带隙和较高的电导率。
> 
> When integrating devices, considering the stacking form of h-BN and its interaction with substrates was crucial [145]. 在集成器件时，考虑h-BN的堆垛形式及其与衬底的相互作用至关重要 [145]。
> 
> The h-BN obtained using mechanical stripping and CVD preparation produces different forms of interlayer sliding, which stems from the number of layers and structural differences in the mechanism of sliding. 使用机械剥离和CVD制备获得的h-BN会产生不同形式的层间滑动，这源于层数和滑动机制的结构差异。
> 
> Lv et al. used a mechanical exfoliation method to prepare bilayer and trilayer h-BN devices with graphene as a substrate and found that the interaction between h-BN and graphene facilitated resistance state switching under an external electric field. Lv等人使用机械剥离法制备了以石墨烯为衬底的双层和三层h-BN器件，发现h-BN与石墨烯之间的相互作用促进了外电场下的电阻态转换。
> 
> The motion of the domain wall under different conditions is shown in Fig. 10a-d. 不同条件下畴壁的运动如图10a-d所示。
> 
> The contact between the substrate and h-BN, specifically B-C and N-C, aided resistance state switching, with graphene as a substrate promoting interlayer sliding ferroelectric behavior between h-BN layers [115]. 衬底与h-BN之间的接触，特别是B-C和N-C，有助于电阻态转换，以石墨烯为衬底促进了h-BN层间的层间滑动铁电行为 [115]。
> 
> In the use of CVD preparation of different structures of h-BN. 在采用CVD制备不同结构的h-BN时。
> 
> The different growth methods were induced to produce diverse structures such as r-BN. 不同的生长方法被诱导产生不同的结构，如r-BN。
> 
> The sliding ferroelectricity induced by the different prepared approaches was different, r-BN had the superior performance to h-BN, which provided an effective way to accurately stack 2D layers of single crystals via coordinating the orientation and sliding vectors of the lattice of each layer through growth. 不同制备方法诱导的滑动铁电性不同，r-BN具有优于h-BN的性能，这为通过生长协调每层晶格的取向和滑动矢量，精确堆垛二维单晶层提供了有效途径。
> 
> The ABC stacking performed by rotating the induced sliding instance with integer multiples of the usual bond lengths, the non-centrosymmetric structure of the ABC stacking of r-BN results in the displacement of charge and spontaneous polarization at the interface, leading to interlayer sliding ferroelectricity (Fig. 10e-h) [116]. 通过以通常键长的整数倍旋转诱导滑动实例进行的ABC堆垛，r-BN的ABC堆垛的非中心对称结构导致界面处的电荷位移和自发极化，从而产生层间滑动铁电性（图10e-h） [116]。
> 
> Interlayers were intercalated to induce sliding ferroelectricity generation. 通过插层来诱导滑动铁电性的产生。
> 
> Graphene, with its strict centrosymmetric, could produce electric dipole layers at multiple interfacial contacts when intercalated between h-BN layers [146]. 具有严格中心对称性的石墨烯，当插入h-BN层之间时，可以在多个界面接触处产生电偶极层 [146]。
> 
> The number of layers and the type of material of the insertion layer may have an impact on the lattice coupling of the original material and thus on the direction of sliding and the energy barrier of sliding. 插入层的层数和材料类型可能对原始材料的晶格耦合产生影响，从而影响滑动方向和滑动能垒。
> 
> Yang et al. proposed that the strict centrosymmetric of hexagonal lattices in both h-BN and graphene, with double-layer graphene embedded between h-BN layers, leads to multilayer sliding and reconstruction of electric dipole layer structures. Yang等人提出，h-BN和石墨烯中六方晶格的严格中心对称性，加上双层石墨烯嵌入h-BN层之间，导致多层滑动和电偶极层结构的重建。
> 
> Due to the lattice constant discrepancy between h-BN and graphene, asymmetric coupling between layers could induce cross-layer sliding, and switching polarization, a mechanism applicable to ferroelectric memory storage [147]. 由于h-BN和石墨烯之间的晶格常数差异，层间不对称耦合可以诱导跨层滑动和极化翻转，这是一种适用于铁电存储器的机制 [147]。
> 
> When h-BN is in a twist, the lattice relaxes to form a superlattice. 当h-BN处于扭转状态时，晶格弛豫形成超晶格。
> 
> During twisting, the emergence of a twist angle in h-BN breaks the symmetry, with lateral displacement of a lattice site within domains causing coupled lateral sliding. 在扭转过程中，h-BN中扭转角的出现打破了对称性，畴内晶格位置的横向位移导致耦合的横向滑动。
> 
> It might be suggested that during the sliding formation of the superlattice, at the 0.24° twist, the relaxation process of the lattice undergoes reconfiguration [29,115]. 可能表明，在超晶格的滑动形成过程中，在0.24°扭转时，晶格的弛豫过程经历了重构 [29,115]。
> 
> It has been demonstrated that long periodic lattices may form during twisting at larger angles, resulting in a reduction in the bandgap. 已经证明，在较大角度扭转期间可能会形成长周期晶格，导致带隙减小。
> 
> Woods et al. found that even minor twist angles between two h-BN crystals, following parallel and antiparallel reconstruction, could induce strong interfacial charge polarization [30]. Woods等人发现，即使是两个h-BN晶体之间微小的扭转角，在平行和反平行重构之后，也能诱导强烈的界面电荷极化 [30]。
> 
> Further study has not been limited to simple interlayer and parallel sequences between h-BN and graphene but this study has explored complex interactions arising from establishing twist angles between h-BN and graphene. 进一步的研究不限于h-BN和石墨烯之间简单的层间和平行序列，而是探索了在h-BN和石墨烯之间建立扭转角所产生的复杂相互作用。
> 
> Niu and his team discovered that forming Moiré superlattices at 0° and 30° twist angles, the Moiré features and Landau theory charge densities reach up to 10¹³ cm⁻², vastly exceeding the capacity of Moiré band structures. Niu及其团队发现，在0°和30°扭转角下形成莫尔超晶格，莫尔特征和朗道理论电荷密度高达10¹³ cm⁻²，远超莫尔能带结构的能力。
> 
> In this study, the combined effects of electron dynamics and lattice distortions under this scenario alter the pinning effect and facilitated sliding [139]. 在这项研究中，电子动力学和晶格畸变在这种情况下的共同作用改变了钉扎效应并促进了滑动 [139]。
> 
> In the 3R stacked h-BN system, the corresponding IP component of polarization exists in the strain and twisted double layer of the symmetry-breaking system, the Moiré superlattice was formed by twisting each other, and the introduction of strain in the interlayer was also a way of generating the Moiré superlattice, and the symmetry breaking after the twist, the polarization arises from the process of IP polarization, the separation of the interlayer produces the cloudy pattern, and the domain wall was correspondingly in the motion of the applied electric field. 在3R堆垛h-BN体系中，相应的面内极化分量存在于对称性破缺体系的应变和扭转双层中，莫尔超晶格通过相互扭转形成，在层间引入应变也是产生莫尔超晶格的一种方式，扭转后对称性破缺，极化来源于面内极化过程，层间分离产生云纹图案，畴壁相应地在外加电场下运动。
> 
> The polar domains were formed in the interdomain relaxation, and during the movement of the polarization, the change of the electric field outside the facet and the change of the polarization inside the facet were measured. 极性畴在畴间弛豫中形成，在极化运动过程中，测量了面外电场的变化和面内极化的变化。
> 
> Determine the direction of sliding, measure the change of polarization in the surface, distinguish the mechanism of sliding generation, and improve the ability of information processing for generating laminar systems [148]. 确定滑动方向，测量面内极化变化，区分滑动产生机制，并提高产生层状系统的信息处理能力 [148]。
>

>

(g) PFM signals at different temperatures indicating the Curie temperature. (h) Amplitude and phase loops of PFM measurements at different bias states [116]. Copyright 2024, Springer Nature. 图10. > 
> ## 4.3. TMDs
> 
> ## 4.3. 过渡金属硫族化合物
> 
> Due to the difference in sliding ferroelectricity in different structures of TMDs with the format of MX2 (M = Mo, W; X = S, Se, Te), there are corresponding variations in the causes of sliding ferroelectrics. 由于TMDs不同结构中滑动铁电性的差异，形式为MX2 (M = Mo, W; X = S, Se, Te)，滑动铁电体的成因也存在相应变化。
> 
> In the 1T, 2H, and 3R construction of TMDs, the 3R configuration has a noncentrosymmetric structure that could induce the generation of a sliding ferroelectricity electrode state. 在TMDs的1T、2H和3R构造中，3R构型具有非中心对称结构，可以诱导产生滑动铁电性电极态。
> 
> In the 2H-type of TMDs, the disruption of central symmetry was a condition for the generation of sliding ferroelectricity, and the establishment of the twist angle to form a superlattice. 在2H型TMDs中，中心对称性的破坏是产生滑动铁电性的条件，并建立扭转角以形成超晶格。
> 
> Wang et al. found that non-smooth domain wall motion through stroboscopic measurements of domain wall velocities in 1T-TaS2 using TEM, where the domain walls move during sliding, and step-sliding produces superlattices, the appearance of which indicates a loss of symmetry due to the appearance of stacked interiors [119]. Wang等人通过使用TEM对1T-TaS2中畴壁速度进行频闪测量，发现畴壁运动不光滑，其中畴壁在滑动过程中移动，步进滑动产生超晶格，超晶格的出现表明由于堆垛内部的出现而失去对称性 [119]。
> 
> The deformed structure of 1T, which produces a transition on the lattice, was a common cause of sliding ferroelectricity generation. 1T的变形结构在晶格上产生转变，是滑动铁电性产生的常见原因。
> 
> Under certain conditions, 1T could be converted to 2H and 3R types. 在某些条件下，1T可以转化为2H和3R类型。
> 
> For instance, the structure phase of the 2H type could be transformed into the 1T configuration under laser irradiation. 例如，2H型的结构相可以在激光照射下转变为1T构型。
> 
> This transition was triggered by vacancies in the tellurium atom while the switching operations of domains and domain walls further confirmed the reversible toggling capability of ferroelectric polarization. 这种转变是由碲原子中的空位触发的，而畴和畴壁的翻转操作进一步证实了铁电极化的可逆翻转能力。
> 
> This process revealed the response mechanism of the material to external stimuli [149]. 这个过程揭示了材料对外部刺激的响应机制 [149]。
> 
> Different stacking sequences generate sliding between layers in different ways, and spontaneous symmetry breaking in the 1T phase could be achieved by establishing a deformation structure in the form of 1T’. 不同的堆垛顺序以不同方式产生层间滑动，1T相中的自发对称性破缺可以通过建立1T'形式的变形结构来实现。
> 
> MoS2 has a triple axis of symmetry, and the occurrence of structural distortions could generate a switchable polarized monolayer ferroelectricity, which was effective in breaking the symmetry structure [150]. MoS2具有三重对称轴，结构畸变的发生可以产生可翻转的极化单层铁电性，这有效打破了对称结构 [150]。
> 
> However, 2H and 3R configurations for multilayer stacking, vdW materials had no dangling bonds, the weak interlayer forces made the resistance to sliding less, and there was a higher probability of interlayer displacements being able to take place, with switchable interlayer stacking configurations. 然而，对于多层堆垛的2H和3R构型，范德华材料没有悬挂键，弱的层间力使滑动阻力减小，层间位移发生的概率更高，具有可翻转的层间堆垛构型。
> 
> The OOP polarization was able to be inverted by the IP sliding, creating almost parallel stacked bilayers. 面外极化可以通过面内滑动反转，形成几乎平行的堆垛双层。
> 
> The TMDs with different atomic compositions and their configurations affect the specific manifestation and mechanism of sliding ferroelectricity. 具有不同原子组成的TMDs及其构型会影响滑动铁电性的具体表现和机制。
> 
> For example, different TMDs exhibited different degrees of sliding ferroelectricity due to the different electron arrangements and interaction forces between their atoms. 例如，不同的TMDs由于其原子间不同的电子排布和相互作用力，表现出不同程度的滑动铁电性。
> 
> In addition, the stacking mode of the materials (e.g., AB stacking, AA’ stacking, etc.) will also have an important effect on their ferroelectric properties. 此外，材料的堆垛模式（例如，AB堆垛、AA'堆垛等）也会对其铁电特性产生重要影响。
> 
> This ability to control interlayer sliding opens up new possibilities for the development of novel electronic and optoelectronic devices such as non-volatile memories, sensors, and logic devices. 这种控制层间滑动的能力为开发新型电子和光电器件（如非易失性存储器、传感器和逻辑器件）开辟了新的可能性。
> 
> By precisely controlling the stacking configuration and interlayer interactions of TMDs, their electronic properties could be tailored, opening up a range of applications based on 2D materials [151]. 通过精确控制TMDs的堆垛构型和层间相互作用，可以定制其电子特性，开辟一系列基于二维材料的应用 [151]。
> 
> Among the myriad of 2D materials, TMDs exhibited pronounced layer-dependent characteristics. 在众多的二维材料中，TMDs表现出明显的层数依赖特性。
> 
> This dependency was closely related to the number of interlayer stackings and the count of internal dipole layers within the material. 这种依赖性与层间堆垛的数量和材料内部偶极层的数量密切相关。
> 
> Significantly, in the study of TMDs, precise control over the number of layers was particularly crucial, as there were significant differences in the material properties between odd and even numbers of layers. 值得注意的是，在TMDs的研究中，精确控制层数尤为关键，因为奇数和偶数层之间的材料特性存在显著差异。
> 
> During the fabrication of dual-gate transistors, the number of layers not only determined the number of stable states but also directly affected the magnitude of spontaneous polarization. 在制造双栅晶体管期间，层数不仅决定了稳定状态的数量，还直接影响自发极化的大小。
> 
> In the 2H phase MoS2, even-numbered layers maintained basic inversion symmetry, while odd-numbered layers exhibited mirror symmetry. 在2H相MoS2中，偶数层保持基本的反演对称性，而奇数层表现出镜面对称性。
> 
> However, in the 3R phase MoS2, both inversion and mirror symmetries were disrupted, a unique symmetry breaking that allowed for charge transfer between adjacent atoms. 然而，在3R相MoS2中，反演对称性和镜面对称性都被破坏，这是一种独特的对称性破缺，允许相邻原子之间发生电荷转移。
> 
> In the stacking configurations of three and four layers, a greater number of polarization state transitions were observed. 在三层和四层的堆垛构型中，观察到更多的极化状态转变。
> 
> In the systems with three layers and their multiples, during the sliding process, the entire system tended to transition towards the state of lowest energy, indicating that a single sliding plane was relatively more dynamically stable. 在三层及其倍数的体系中，在滑动过程中，整个体系倾向于向能量最低的状态转变，这表明单个滑动平面相对更动态稳定。
> 
> In-depth studies on four and five-layer 2D ferroelectric materials had led to the proposal of a generalized model, which suggested that in multilayer systems, there was typically only one active sliding plane, and under the condition of ensuring the lowest total energy of the system, only n-1 layers participated in sliding. 对四层和五层二维铁电材料的深入研究导致提出了一个广义模型，该模型表明，在多層体系中，通常只有一个活跃的滑动平面，并且在确保体系总能量最低的条件下，只有n-1层参与滑动。
> 
> During the sliding process, the material undergoes a series of intermediate state transitions, thereby reaching a new equilibrium state (Fig. 11a and c) [3]. 在滑动过程中，材料经历一系列中间状态转变，从而达到新的平衡状态（图11a和c） [3]。
> 
> In their study on TMDs, Deb et al. delineated two distinct phenomena: OOP and IP polarization. 在对TMDs的研究中，Deb等人描述了两种不同的现象：面外和面内极化。
> 
> OOP polarization raised from the intrinsic symmetry breaking at interfaces, while IP polarization resulted from the interactions among carriers within the 2D material, manifesting in a preferred polarization state. 面外极化源于界面处的本征对称性破缺，而面内极化源于二维材料内载流子之间的相互作用，表现为一种优先的极化状态。
> 
> The phenomenon of sliding ferroelectricity provided an effective means to control OOP polarization. 滑动铁电现象为控制面外极化提供了有效手段。
> 
> In supplementary research on TMD systems, particularly within the trilayer structure of WSe2, manipulation of layers with opposing polarization directions facilitated the formation of rhombohedral stacking configurations, such as ABC and CBA types. 在对TMD体系的补充研究中，特别是在WSe2的三层结构内，操纵具有相反极化方向的层促进了菱方堆垛构型的形成，如ABC和CBA类型。
> 
> Moreover, in the study of trilayer MoS2 stacking, the introduction of relative displacements between layers in localized configurations effectively promoted the generation of interface cumulative effects, thereby enabling precise control over material properties [51]. 此外，在三层MoS2堆垛的研究中，在局域构型中引入层间相对位移，有效促进了界面累积效应的产生，从而能够精确控制材料特性 [51]。
> 
> In the investigation of sliding ferroelectricity within different-layer configurations of TMDs, significant emphasis has been placed on delineating the relationship between sliding ferroelectric phenomena and photonic behaviors. 在TMDs不同层数构型中滑动铁电性的研究中，重点阐述了滑动铁电现象与光子行为之间的关系。
> 
> The study extended to an analysis of phonon interactions within both quadruple and bilayer constructs, wherein the response to interlayer sliding manifested predominantly through chiral phonons along the X and Y axes in bilayer systems. 研究扩展到对四层和双层结构内声子相互作用的分析，其中对层间滑动的响应主要通过双层体系中沿X和Y轴的手性声子表现出来。
> 
> **Chiral phonon**: 手性声子，具有特定手性（旋转方向）的晶格振动模式，与磁性、谷极化等物理性质相关。
> 
> This phonon response to interlayer sliding exhibited parallels to the intrinsic phonon behavior, suggesting that the act of sliding between layers exerted a tangible influence on photonic characteristics and Berry curvature, thereby yielding notable effects within the material system. 这种声子对层间滑动的响应表现出与本征声子行为的相似性，表明层间滑动行为对光子特性和贝里曲率产生了切实影响，从而在材料体系中产生显著效应。
> 
> **Berry curvature**: 贝里曲率，描述电子波函数在动量空间中几何性质的物理量，与反常霍尔效应、谷霍尔效应等拓扑性质密切相关。
> 
> This exploration contributed to a more nuanced understanding of the interplay between mechanical displacements within layered structures and their resultant impact on electronic and photonic properties [152]. 这一探索有助于更细致地理解层状结构内机械位移与其对电子和光子特性最终影响之间的相互作用 [152]。
> 
> By meticulously controlling the number of layers to manipulate polarization properties, the method of introducing twist angles within multilayer structures has been employed to construct polarization modes. 通过精心控制层数来操纵极化特性，已采用在多层结构中引入扭转角的方法来构建极化模式。
> 
> The twisting between two identical lattices with minute twist angles, especially under conditions of lattice mismatch, could induce the formation of Moiré superlattices. 两个相同晶格之间以微小扭转角扭转，特别是在晶格失配的条件下，可以诱导莫尔超晶格的形成。
> 
> Wu et al. created Moiré superlattice structures by introducing twist angles of 56.83° and 3.15° between layers. Wu等人通过在层间引入56.83°和3.15°的扭转角创建了莫尔超晶格结构。
> 
> In the four-layer structure of MoS2, such twisting not only facilitated the formation of the lattice but also introduced coupling between polarized states and related states. 在MoS2的四层结构中，这种扭转不仅促进了晶格的形成，而且引入了极化态和相关态之间的耦合。
> 
> By controlling the twist angle between the first and second layers or between the third and fourth layers, the characteristics of the Moiré superlattices could be precisely adjusted, significantly impacting ferroelectric polarization. 通过控制第一层和第二层之间或第三层和第四层之间的扭转角，可以精确调整莫尔超晶格的特性，显著影响铁电极化。
> 
> Through the control of stacking order and twist angles, effective modulation of the ferroelectric polarization and its associated states coupling could be achieved [153]. 通过控制堆垛顺序和扭转角，可以实现对铁电极化及其相关态耦合的有效调制 [153]。
> 
> The exploration of the mechanisms of lattice and domain wall motion formed during the sliding process. 探索滑动过程中形成的晶格和畴壁运动机制。
> 
> Polarization switching was difficult to implement in single-domain devices [31]. 在单畴器件中，极化翻转难以实现 [31]。
> 
> In the design of twisted heterostructures of MoS2, the interfacial charge transfer and domain wall motion between monolayer and bilayer configurations were leveraged. 在MoS2扭转异质结的设计中，利用了单层和双层构型之间的界面电荷转移和畴壁运动。
> 
> The introduction of twisting at the domain walls and pinning effects within the lattice symmetry led to the formation of Moiré superlattices. 在畴壁处引入扭转以及晶格对称性内的钉扎效应导致莫尔超晶格的形成。
> 
> This process involved atomic periodic variations and explored the relationships for small local twist angles. 这个过程涉及原子周期性变化，并探索了局部小扭转角的关系。
> 
> The network structure of domains and the parallel stacking between homogenous layers facilitated the construction of a 3R configuration. 畴的网络结构和均匀层之间的平行堆垛促进了3R构型的构建。
> 
> Angles 0 < θ < 2°, 3R lattice domains were formed, contrasting with the antiparallel configuration of the 2H phase. 在角度0 < θ < 2°范围内，形成3R晶格畴，与2H相的反平行构型形成对比。
> 
> In the context of the 3R bilayer, the lack of inversion centers and mirror symmetry marked 3R as a non-centrosymmetric structure. 在3R双层的背景下，缺乏反演中心和镜面对称性标志着3R是非中心对称结构。
> 
> Post-twisting, the asymmetrical hybridization between valence bands led to interlayer charge transfer, which was conducive to OOP dipole moments in terms of polarization coupling [32]. 扭转后，价带之间的不对称杂化导致层间电荷转移，这有利于极化耦合方面的面外偶极矩 [32]。
> 
> Based on the ferroelectric properties generated by the twisting and sliding mechanisms between layers, materials with sliding ferroelectricity were successfully predicted using DFT principles. 基于层间扭转和滑动机制产生的铁电特性，利用DFT原理成功预测了具有滑动铁电性的材料。
> 
> The verification of ferroelectric polarization reversal was achieved using reverse packaging techniques and the application of an external electric field. 铁电极化翻转的验证是通过反向封装技术和施加外部电场实现的。
> 
> Lipatov et al. employed a direct observation method using the mechanical pressure induced by the tip of a scanning probe microscope for polarization switching. Lipatov等人采用了一种直接观察方法，利用扫描探针显微镜针尖诱导的机械压力进行极化翻转。
> 
> Specifically, in the study of the 1T phase, the observation of the distorted structure of 1T-MoS2 confirmed the presence of its ferroelectric properties [154]. 具体来说，在1T相的研究中，对1T-MoS2变形结构的观察证实了其铁电特性的存在 [154]。
> 
> Yang et al. found that the release of domain walls in single crystals, which allows polarization switching in different stacking modes, and experimentally demonstrated that switching occurs by applying an electric field in AB and BA stacks for sliding, and by applying a larger positive magnetic field for switching of polarization, and MoS2 itself had the triple axis of symmetry polarization of the switching had an uncertainty in the mechanical stripping of the single crystals produced by the probability of the larger, single-crystal domain walls in the sliding ferroelectric effectively controlled the polarization switching and applied it (Fig. 11d) [19]. Yang等人发现，单晶中畴壁的释放允许不同堆垛模式下的极化翻转，并通过实验证明，在AB和BA堆垛中施加电场进行滑动可实现翻转，而施加较大的正磁场可实现极化翻转，MoS2本身具有三重对称轴，机械剥离单晶产生的极化翻转概率较大，单晶畴壁在滑动铁电体中有效控制了极化翻转并将其应用（图11d） [19]。
> 
> There was another way to illustrate the sliding ferroelectric in atomic level dynamics, the covalent bonding of atoms was introduced in TMDs, and the vdW forces and covalent bonds cause interlayer fluctuations, leading to localized sliding of the layers. 还有另一种方式来说明原子级动力学中的滑动铁电性，在TMDs中引入了原子的共价键，范德华力和共价键导致层间涨落，从而导致层的局部滑动。
> 
> Embedding of atoms was carried out within the 2D material to break the original energy balance, the dipoles of the layers were aligned in the OOP direction, and the reduction in the potential barriers for the sliding of the layers ensured that the ferroelectric domains could be switched in the presence of an applied electric field [155]. 在二维材料内部进行原子嵌入以打破原始能量平衡，层的偶极子在面外方向排列，层滑动势垒的降低确保铁电畴在存在外加电场时可以翻转 [155]。
> 
> The WSe2 was identified as a material exhibiting ambipolar characteristics. WSe2被确定为一种表现出双极性特性的材料。
> 
> In the study of multilayer stacking structures, Winkle et al. demonstrated that non-centrosymmetric structures could be constructed through layer-by-layer assembly, where interfacial polarization was enhanced by the cumulative translational offsets within the multilayer configuration. 在多层堆垛结构的研究中，Winkle等人证明，可以通过逐层组装构建非中心对称结构，其中界面极化通过多层构型内的累积平移偏移得到增强。
> 
> Additionally, the pinning effect at domain walls was utilized to control the switching properties in multilayer stacks. 此外，利用畴壁处的钉扎效应来控制多层堆垛中的翻转特性。
> 
> The formation of Moiré lattices was observed in three-layer stacked structures, and the spontaneous relaxation of Moiré superlattices was achieved by controlling the twist angle. 在三层堆垛结构中观察到莫尔晶格的形成，通过控制扭转角实现了莫尔超晶格的自发弛豫。
> 
> TEM techniques have been employed to study the impact of rotation in the middle and top layers on the superlattice, revealing differences in superlattice structures generated by the rotation of the middle layer compared to the top layer. 已采用TEM技术研究中间层和顶层旋转对超晶格的影响，揭示中间层旋转与顶层旋转产生的超晶格结构的差异。
> 
> By altering the relative rotation of the uniaxial strain fields within and between layers, the structure of the middle layer could be modified, thus achieving a preferred polarization state. 通过改变层内和层间单轴应变场的相对旋转，可以改变中间层的结构，从而实现优选的极化状态。
> 
> In multilayer systems, the order of polarity and switching responsiveness were enhanced compared to bilayer structures. 在多层体系中，与双层结构相比，极性顺序和翻转响应性得到增强。
> 
> The accumulation of interfacial polarization and the influence of rotation on Moiré superlattices underscored the importance of structural fine-tuning in the design of advanced functional materials (Fig. 11e) [120]. 界面极化的积累和旋转对莫尔超晶格的影响凸显了结构微调在先进功能材料设计中的重要性（图11e） [120]。
> 
> Introducing covalent bonding on atoms was also a way for TMDs to produce sliding ferroelectricity. 在原子间引入共价键也是TMDs产生滑动铁电性的一种方式。
> 
> During the growth process of trilayer deposition, an OOP ferroelectric polarization response was observed. 在三层沉积的生长过程中，观察到面外铁电极化响应。
> 
> The surface structures of monolayers and trilayers exhibited non-polar symmetric configurations, whereas bilayers manifested non-centrosymmetric structures, characterized by pronounced SHG signal responses. 单层和三层的表面结构表现出非极性对称构型，而双层表现出非中心对称结构，以显著的SHG信号响应为特征。
> 
> Moreover, in the realm of topological materials survey, the manipulation of polar domain walls using electron beams has been employed to disrupt both time-reversal and spatial inversion symmetries. 此外，在拓扑材料研究领域，已采用电子束操纵极性畴壁来破坏时间反演和空间反演对称性。
> 
> Advanced imaging techniques, including TEM, have been utilized in the investigation of ferroelectricity to reveal conductive interface states [156]. 包括TEM在内的先进成像技术已被用于铁电性研究，以揭示导电界面态 [156]。
> 
> In addition to the WSe2, the discovery of WTe2 and the structure of 1T’-WTe2 revealed that polarization originated from the IP translational sliding between layers, which enabled switching. 除了WSe2，WTe2和1T’-WTe2结构的发现揭示了极化源于层间面内平移滑动，这使得翻转成为可能。
> 
> The interlayer ferroelectricity limit can be reached up to a single atomic layer due to spontaneous symmetry breaking triggered by atomic displacements of MoTe2. 由于MoTe2原子位移引发的自发对称性破缺，层间铁电性极限可以达到单原子层。
> 
> The exploration of trilayer TMDs with a focus on MoTe2, and the synthesis of multilayer MoTe2 single crystals has been conducted [157, 158]. 对以MoTe2为重点的三层TMDs的探索以及多层MoTe2单晶的合成已经进行 [157, 158]。
> 
> The ferroelectricity in bilayer WTe2 arises from the transfer of interlayer charges between the bilayers. 双层WTe2中的铁电性源于双层之间层间电荷的转移。
> 
> The impact of lattice strain on the bilayer structure, particularly within the spin texture, which was subjected to exploration under biaxial strain. 晶格应变对双层结构的影响，特别是在自旋纹理内，在双轴应变下进行了探索。
> 
> Ferroelectric variability could be investigated under a 3 % tensile strain, which was found to enhance polarization, while a 3 % compressive strain could transition bilayer WTe2 from a ferroelectric to a paraelectric phase. 在3%的拉伸应变下可以研究铁电可变性，发现其增强了极化，而3%的压缩应变可以将双层WTe2从铁电相转变为顺电相。
> 
> The exploration of ferroelectricity and its reversal in bilayers underscored that ferroelectricity stemmed from the transfer of interlayer charges during the sliding process [123]. 对双层中铁电性及其翻转的探索强调了铁电性源于滑动过程中层间电荷的转移 [123]。
> 
> Monolayer WTe2 exhibited centrosymmetric, categorizing it as a non-polar material. 单层WTe2表现出中心对称性，将其归类为非极性材料。
> 
> There was a specific relationship between the charge and spin texture in WTe2, which facilitated non-destructive readout capability when utilized in devices [159]. WTe2中电荷和自旋纹理之间存在特定关系，这有助于在器件中实现无损读出能力 [159]。
> 
> In the investigation of ferroelectric behavior within bilayer and trilayer WTe2 structures, a distinctive distribution of vertical charges between bi-molecular layers was identified. 在双层和三层WTe2结构内铁电行为的研究中，确定了双分子层之间垂直电荷的独特分布。
> 
> The driving force behind spontaneous polarization in WTe2 structures, consisting of two or more layers, was attributed to the displacement of electron clouds from their nuclei. WTe2结构（由两层或多层组成）中自发极化背后的驱动力归因于电子云偏离其原子核的位移。
> 
> Notably, this displacement was not a result of lattice distortion. 值得注意的是，这种位移不是晶格畸变的结果。
> 
> Instead, polarization originated from the transfer of uncompensated charges between layers, Fig. 11b shows the steady-state curves of three-layer WTe2 devices in an applied electric field facilitated by IP sliding mechanisms [20,122]. 相反，极化源于层间未补偿电荷的转移，图11b显示了三层WTe2器件在由面内滑动机制促进的外加电场下的稳态曲线 [20,122]。
> 
> The vdW materials had independent switchable electrode polarization and showed significant hysteresis effects in PFM tests [29,160]. 范德华材料具有独立可翻转的电极极化，并在PFM测试中显示出显著的滞后效应 [29,160]。
> 
> Structures exhibiting switchable spontaneous polarization and intrinsic ferroelectric domains at room temperature have been effectively correlated with bilayer and trilayer WTe2 through sliding plane operations. 在室温下表现出可翻转自发极化和本征铁电畴的结构，已通过滑动面操作与双层和三层WTe2有效关联。
> 
> The interlayer sliding induces interactions between dipole moments, while the OOP electric field triggers an internal layer framework sliding reversal, manifesting in a similar polarization behavior. 层间滑动诱导偶极矩之间的相互作用，而面外电场触发内部层框架滑动翻转，表现出类似的极化行为。
> 
> This phenomenon underscores the intricate relationship between mechanical displacements within the layered structure and resultant ferroelectric properties, highlighting the potential for controlled ferroelectric switching in nanoscale devices [99,161]. 这一现象强调了层状结构内机械位移与由此产生的铁电特性之间的复杂关系，突显了在纳米尺度器件中可控铁电翻转的潜力 [99,161]。
> 
> Jinda et al. expanded their study on bilayer molecular structures, focusing on the driving forces of inter-band pairing interactions within the layered structure of 2D sliding ferroelectrics. Jinda等人扩展了他们对双层分子结构的研究，重点关注二维滑动铁电体层状结构内带间配对相互作用的驱动力。
> 
> In WTe2, superconductivity emerged only under conditions where both electrons and holes coexist. 在WTe2中，超导性仅在电子和空穴共存的条件下出现。
> 
> As a material with significant degrees of sliding freedom, WTe2 offered a promising platform for exploring the interplay between superconducting states and polarization. 作为一种具有显著滑动自由度的材料，WTe2为探索超导态与极化之间的相互作用提供了一个有前景的平台。
> 
> The control of 2D superconductivity through doping and displacement fields presented a novel approach. 通过掺杂和位移场控制二维超导性提供了一种新颖的方法。
> 
> Investigating the relationship between layer thickness and twist angles will likely chart the future course for this material’s development, potentially unveiling new avenues in the study of superconductivity and ferroelectricity [157]. 研究层厚与扭转角之间的关系可能会为该材料的未来发展指明方向，有可能揭示超导性和铁电性研究的新途径 [157]。
> 
> In the study of polarization phenomena induced between bilayer molecules by laser irradiation, bilayer WTe2 emerged as a paradigmatic 2D sliding ferroelectric material. 在激光辐照诱导双层分子间极化现象的研究中，双层WTe2成为典型的二维滑动铁电材料。
> 
> The concurrent processes of sliding and compression between layers facilitate the transfer of interlayer dipoles and charges. 层间滑动和压缩的并发过程促进了层间偶极子和电荷的转移。
> 
> The interaction of dipoles leads to a piezoelectric effect induced by laser-promoted interlayer compression, achieving the modulation of ferroelectric properties in layered materials. 偶极子的相互作用导致激光促进的层间压缩诱导的压电效应，实现了层状材料中铁电特性的调制。
> 
> The electronic response of the bilayer was characterized by pulses of red light that were axially polarized, perpendicular to the plane. 双层的电子响应由轴向偏振的红光脉冲表征，垂直于平面。
> 
> Computational analysis revealed an enhancement in the interlayer coupling, with the potential energy increasing as the interlayer distance diminishes. 计算分析显示层间耦合增强，势能随着层间距减小而增加。
> 
> The presence of ferroelectric polarization was evidenced by the simultaneous action of interlayer compression and sliding induced by laser irradiation, underscoring the complex interplay between mechanical and electrical phenomena in 2D ferroelectrics. 铁电极化的存在由激光辐照诱导的层间压缩和滑动的同时作用证明，强调了二维铁电体中机械和电现象之间复杂的相互作用。
> 
> IP sliding induced by OOP electric field and electrostatic doping, the existence of layer dependence in WTe2, an inversion operation for odd layers and a mirror operation for even layers, the correlation between Berry curvature dipoles and ferroelectric polarization induced by interlayer sliding, and the exploration of correlated degrees of freedom for the interactions between higher-order topologies become possible [28]. 面外电场和静电掺杂诱导的面内滑动，WTe2中层数依赖性的存在，奇数层的反演操作和偶数层的镜面操作，贝里曲率偶极子与层间滑动诱导的铁电极化之间的关联，以及探索高阶拓扑之间相互作用的相关自由度成为可能 [28]。
> 
> Within the context of topological metals, the ultrafast ferroelectric ordering remained unshielded by the mobile electrons inherent to metals, a phenomenon that did not impede the manifestation of electric polarization. 在拓扑金属的背景下，超快铁电有序保持不受金属固有移动电子的屏蔽，这种现象并未阻碍电极化的表现。
> 
> This unique behavior was underpinned by the distribution of uncompensated charges associated with interlayer shear modes, which actuated and drove structural phase transitions. 这种独特行为由与层间剪切模式相关的未补偿电荷分布支撑，这些剪切模式启动并驱动了结构相变。
> 
> Such charge distributions led to the emergence of normal polarization, thereby modulating the lattice symmetry. 这种电荷分布导致正常极化的出现，从而调制了晶格对称性。
> 
> This interplay elucidated the intricate dynamics between electronic and structural properties in topological metals, offering a nuanced understanding of their ferroelectric characteristics [162]. 这种相互作用阐明了拓扑金属中电子和结构特性之间的复杂动力学，为理解其铁电特性提供了细致入微的见解 [162]。

> 
> ## 4.4. MX
> 
> ## 4.4. MX
> 
> In the MX (where M = Ge, Sn; X = S, Se) system, a reversible transition between ferroelectricity and antiferroelectricity is induced to occur internally. 在MX（其中M = Ge, Sn；X = S, Se）体系中，铁电性和反铁电性之间的可逆转变被诱导在内部发生。
> 
> **Antiferroelectricity**: 反铁电性，指相邻偶极子反平行排列，宏观上不显示净极化的状态，但在外电场下可转变为铁电相。
> 
> This transition mainly originates from the micro displacement of interlayer atoms and the modulation of interlayer vdW interactions induced by doping, which was analyzed at the lattice level, where the interaction of vdW forces between the bilayers leads to a distortion of the lattice, and this distortion further affects the breaking and formation of covalent bonds within the layers. 这种转变主要源于层间原子的微观位移和掺杂诱导的层间范德华相互作用的调制，这在晶格层面进行了分析，其中双层之间范德华力的相互作用导致晶格畸变，这种畸变进一步影响层内共价键的断裂和形成。
> 
> In addition, interlayer sliding induces a significant change in energy. 此外，层间滑动诱导能量的显著变化。
> 
> MX studies of sliding ferroelectricity have mostly focused on the generation mechanism, the number of layers the form of stacking, and the direction of sliding. MX滑动铁电性的研究大多集中在产生机制、层数、堆垛形式和滑动方向。
> 
> In the study of GaSe, non-ferroelectric monolayer materials exhibited ferroelectric properties. 在GaSe的研究中，非铁电单层材料表现出铁电特性。
> 
> In 2D materials like GaSe, the interlayer sliding in multilayer structures, akin to the sliding mechanism in α-In2Se3, could induce ferroelectricity. 在像GaSe这样的二维材料中，多层结构中的层间滑动，类似于α-In2Se3中的滑动机制，可以诱导铁电性。
> 
> The sliding within the Se atomic layers breaks the mirror symmetry of the monolayer structure, leading to the alignment of dipole moments. Se原子层内的滑动打破了单层结构的镜面对称性，导致偶极矩的排列。
> 
> The internal sliding of Se atoms results in ferroelectricity, which could control the IP and OOP polarization in GaSe. Se原子的内部滑动导致铁电性，这可以控制GaSe中的面内和面外极化。
> 
> This could be applied to devices for thousands of cycles. 这可以应用于器件数千次循环。
> 
> The material’s broken symmetry properties were characterized using SHG, indicating the potential presence of ferroelectric polarization. 使用SHG表征了材料的对称性破缺特性，表明可能存在铁电极化。
> 
> OOP polarization was confirmed using PFM during the operation process, with synchronized phase adjustments and simultaneous readings of IP and OOP amplitudes. 在操作过程中使用PFM确认了面外极化，并同步调整相位，同时读取面内和面外振幅。
> 
> The internal sliding of atoms within the monolayer could still occur, maintaining the presence of IP and OOP polarization. 单层内原子的内部滑动仍然可以发生，保持面内和面外极化的存在。
> 
> GaSe is a 2D material used to make FET, and it possesses a high ON/OFF ratio, with atomic polarization capable of polarization reversal, and exhibits good switching properties and repeatability [36]. GaSe是一种用于制造FET的二维材料，具有高开/关比，原子极化能够进行极化翻转，并表现出良好的开关特性和可重复性 [36]。
> 
> Previous studies have suggested some cases to demonstrate the sliding ferroelectric in MX, which could be influenced by stacking and layer numbers. 先前的研究提出了一些案例来证明MX中的滑动铁电性，这可能受到堆垛和层数的影响。
> 
> Xu et al. showed that interlayer vdW interactions outside the facets could significantly affect the distortion of the lattice inside the facets, thus promoting the breaking and formation of covalent bonds between layers. Xu等人表明，面外的层间范德华相互作用可以显著影响面内晶格的畸变，从而促进层间共价键的断裂和形成。
> 
> In the interlayer interactions induced by sliding, AB stacking was transformed into AC stacking by moving 0.5 a (lattice constant) along the x-direction and 0.5 b (lattice constant) in the y-direction, and at the same time, AD stacking could be further formed by moving in the x- and y-directions. 在滑动引起的层间相互作用中，AB堆垛通过沿x方向移动0.5 a（晶格常数）和y方向移动0.5 b（晶格常数）转变为AC堆垛，同时，通过在x和y方向移动可以进一步形成AD堆垛。
> 
> The energy calculations of multiple stacking configurations revealed that the energy trend of ferroelectricity was manifested as AC < AB < AA < AD, in which AA and AC stacking tended to be ferroelectric (Fig. 12a), while AB and AD stacking tended to be in the antiferroelectric state. 多种堆垛构型的能量计算表明，铁电性的能量趋势表现为AC < AB < AA < AD，其中AA和AC堆垛倾向于铁电（图12a），而AB和AD堆垛倾向于反铁电态。
> 
> Interlayer sliding not only changes the stacking order but also activates the transition of polarization states. 层间滑动不仅改变堆垛顺序，还激活极化状态的转变。
> 
> It was worth noting that the energy differences that existed between materials (e.g., SnS, SnSe, GaSe vs. GaS) as well as the energy barriers to be overcome when switching between ferroelectric and antiferroelectric states also differ significantly [55]. 值得注意的是，材料之间存在的能量差异（例如，SnS、SnSe、GaSe与GaS之间）以及在铁电态和反铁电态之间切换时需要克服的能垒也存在显著差异 [55]。
> 
> Li et al.’s exploration of GaSe discovered mirror-symmetric structures showcasing significant IP and OOP interrelated polarization. Li等人对GaSe的探索发现了镜面对称结构，展示了显著的面内和面外相互关联的极化。
> 
> The sliding of Se atoms within mirror-symmetric structures generated electric dipole moments. 镜面对称结构内Se原子的滑动产生电偶极矩。
> 
> Larger polarization phenomena between multilayers decreased towards the limit of single-layer films, with monolayer materials maintaining symmetry. 多层之间较大的极化现象向单层薄膜的极限减小，单层材料保持对称性。
> 
> In 15 nm GaSe applications within non-volatile storage devices, IP and OOP polarizations could be reversed by applying biases to the channel or gate, interrelating the two. 在15 nm GaSe应用于非易失性存储器件中，面内和面外极化可以通过对沟道或栅极施加偏压来翻转，将两者相互关联。
> 
> Moreover, resistance switching exhibited good repeatability, with intralayer sliding being the primary mechanism in multilayer GaSe for application in nanodevice fabrication achieving high current ON/OFF ratios (Fig. 12c and d) [36]. 此外，电阻切换表现出良好的可重复性，层内滑动是多层GaSe应用于纳米器件制造的主要机制，实现了高电流开/关比（图12c和d） [36]。
>

>

> 
> * * *
> 
> ## 4.5. Heterojunction
> 
> ## 4.5. 异质结
> 
> The heterojunction formation has differences in lattice constants, and the free combination of h-BN, graphene, and TMDs gives sufficient freedom for the composition of sliding ferroelectricity. 异质结的形成存在晶格常数差异，h-BN、石墨烯和TMDs的自由组合为滑动铁电性的构成提供了足够的自由度。
> 
> Heterojunctions have grown by CVD or reconstructed by exfoliation, which provides more freedom in the selection of the substrate and the angle of construction compared to conventional materials. 异质结已通过CVD生长或剥离重构，与传统材料相比，这在衬底选择和构建角度方面提供了更多自由度。
> 
> Consequently, the degree of ferroelectric polarization also varies depending on the process of obtaining the heterojunction. 因此，铁电极化程度也因获得异质结的工艺而异。
> 
> Heterojunctions produce Moiré stripes with corresponding polarization in the plane. 异质结产生莫尔条纹，并在平面内具有相应的极化。
> 
> In the MoS2/WS2 heterostructure, symmetry breaking, interlayer sliding, adjustment of twist angles, and Moiré lattice domains were considered. 在MoS2/WS2异质结中，考虑了对称性破缺、层间滑动、扭转角调整和莫尔晶格畴。
> 
> The MoS2/WS2 prepared by CVD produced large-area lateral heterostructures, upon forming a third layer, the lattice symmetry was disrupted. 通过CVD制备的MoS2/WS2产生了大面积横向异质结，在形成第三层时，晶格对称性被破坏。
> 
> TMDs possess a three-fold symmetry axis, with n × 60° periods not producing Moiré cloud patterns. TMDs具有三重对称轴，n × 60°周期不会产生莫尔云纹。
> 
> Within the 0–60° period range, corresponding Moiré lattices were formed, showcasing OOP polarization during interlayer sliding lattice distortion and lateral sliding switching polarization [98]. 在0-60°周期范围内，形成了相应的莫尔晶格，在层间滑动晶格畸变和横向滑动翻转极化过程中展示了面外极化 [98]。
> 
> In different interfaces behind the symmetry center, Akamatsu’s formation of a WSe2/BP heterostructure differs from WSe2 in that BP possesses double symmetry and multiple mirror planes. 在对称中心后面的不同界面中，Akamatsu形成的WSe2/BP异质结与WSe2不同，BP具有双重对称性和多个镜面。
> 
> The interface of WSe2/BP lacks rotational symmetry, and the rotational symmetry between the two was incompatible, exhibiting anisotropy [163]. WSe2/BP的界面缺乏旋转对称性，两者之间的旋转对称性不相容，表现出各向异性 [163]。
> 
> Sun et al. investigated the heterostructure formed by CuInP2S6/GaS2 and demonstrated that the formation of the heterostructure led to a polarization reversal capable of simultaneously driving the switching of ferroelectric sliding, resulting in the formation of a Moiré superlattice. Sun等人研究了CuInP2S6/GaS2形成的异质结，并证明异质结的形成导致极化翻转，能够同时驱动铁电滑动的切换，从而形成莫尔超晶格。
> 
> On this basis, the combination of sliding ferroelectricity and ionic displacement presents a multi-polarization concept with enhanced polarization strength, compensating for the limitations in switching observed in Moiré lattices. 在此基础上，滑动铁电性和离子位移的结合呈现出一种多极化概念，极化强度增强，弥补了在莫尔晶格中观察到的翻转限制。
> 
> When the interlayer electrons reconfigured spatially, a coupling between ionic displacement and sliding occurs. 当层间电子在空间上重新配置时，离子位移和滑动之间发生耦合。
> 
> Under a twist angle of five degrees, it was found that the heterostructure could achieve polarization switching through an increased density of ferroelectric arrays and an electric field [127]. 在五度扭转角下，发现异质结可以通过增加铁电阵列密度和电场来实现极化翻转 [127]。
> 
> In the exploration of graphene and h-BN heterojunctions by Niu et al., the crystal structure and interface at the vdW heterojunction interface were reconstructed. 在Niu等人对石墨烯和h-BN异质结的探索中，范德华异质结界面的晶体结构和界面被重构。
> 
> The interface of h-BN and graphene at a small twist angle (0.69°) unaffected by the lattice, encapsulation of graphene at a 30° twist of the h-BN interface, the formation of a Moiré superlattice at the interface [139]. 在不受晶格影响的小扭转角（0.69°）下，h-BN和石墨烯的界面，在h-BN界面30°扭转下封装石墨烯，在界面处形成莫尔超晶格 [139]。
> 
> The presence of a twist angle enables different stacking forms to exist simultaneously, and for 2D materials, changing the size of the domain and the distance between the layers in the process of performing a twist could affect the effect of the twist [164]. 扭转角的存在使不同的堆垛形式能够同时存在，对于二维材料，在进行扭转的过程中改变畴的尺寸和层间距离会影响扭转的效果 [164]。
> 
> Table 3 shows the relationship between the angle of generation of the twist and ferroelectricity for the typical 2D sliding ferroelectric materials. 表3显示了典型二维滑动铁电材料的扭转产生角度与铁电性之间的关系。
>

> ## 4.6. InSe
> 
> ## 4.6. 硒化铟
> 
> InSe, a vdW layered ferroelectric, utilizes weak interlayer bonding forces for switchable polarization under an external electric field, presenting a unique electronic structure. InSe是一种范德华层状铁电体，利用弱的层间键合力在外加电场下实现可翻转极化，呈现出独特的电子结构。
> 
> Doping InSe with yttrium enhanced OOP and IP ferroelectricity during sliding, with an effective piezoelectric constant of 7.5 pm/V. 用钇掺杂InSe增强了滑动过程中的面外和面内铁电性，有效压电常数为7.5 pm/V。
> 
> Sliding effectively induced distortions, with internal doping of rare earth elements altering the material’s lattice structure through strain and stress constraints. 滑动有效诱导了畸变，稀土元素的内部掺杂通过应变和应力约束改变了材料的晶格结构。
> 
> Doping in γ-InSe enhanced induced IP and OOP polarization, disrupting the bandgap in undoped γ-InSe. γ-InSe中的掺杂增强了诱导的面内和面外极化，破坏了未掺杂γ-InSe中的带隙。
> 
> Interlayer sliding and dislocation movement produced super-plasticity, whereas doping significantly optimized the layered structure, enhancing interlayer carrier transfer and distribution. 层间滑动和位错运动产生了超塑性，而掺杂显著优化了层状结构，增强了层间载流子转移和分布。
> 
> Raman spectrum revealed that doping did not alter the interlayer structure but affected the plasticity during the sliding process. 拉曼光谱显示，掺杂没有改变层间结构，但影响了滑动过程中的塑性。
> 
> γ-InSe, belonging to the c53v point group with non-centrosymmetric, exhibited OOP carrier transfer behavior. γ-InSe属于c53v点群，具有非中心对称性，表现出面外载流子转移行为。
> 
> After doping, IP ferroelectricity was triggered, enhancing spontaneous IP and OOP polarization through structural control [35]. 掺杂后，面内铁电性被触发，通过结构控制增强了自发面内和面外极化 [35]。
> 
> Very recently, further extensions were made for the exploration of doped phases of InSe. 最近，对InSe掺杂相的探索进行了进一步扩展。
> 
> For the study of Y-doped γ-InSe, the structure of the bilayer sliding was studied by Sui et al., compared to graphene crosslayer and multilayer study, the polarization of the bilayer unit sliding at the same time compared to the traditional single-layer sliding. 对于Y掺杂γ-InSe的研究，Sui等人研究了双层滑动的结构，与石墨烯跨层和多层研究相比，双层单元同时滑动的极化与传统单层滑动相比。
> 
> In situ observation under the action of the external electric field, the Y doping could act on the sliding barrier to enhance the polarization and stability, which improved the OOP polarization compared to the previous sliding mechanism, and the existence of multi-polarization in the γ-InSe could make the sliding ferroelectrics for a wider range of applications [37]. 在外加电场作用下的原位观察表明，Y掺杂可以作用于滑动势垒，增强极化和稳定性，这与之前的滑动机制相比提高了面外极化，γ-InSe中多极化的存在可以使滑动铁电体具有更广泛的应用 [37]。
> 
> ## 4.7. Other 2D sliding ferroelectric materials by experimental report
> 
> ## 4.7. 实验报道的其他二维滑动铁电材料
> 
> Quantum confinement could be modulated by interlayer sliding, controlling conductivity and carrier mobility [131]. 量子限域可以通过层间滑动来调制，从而控制电导率和载流子迁移率 [131]。
> 
> Ding et al.’s research on β-ZrI2 suggested that the material’s negative longitudinal piezoelectricity results from enhanced interlayer dipoles, where stretching and compressing strains enhanced polarization phenomena, leading to sliding ferroelectric behavior. Ding等人对β-ZrI2的研究表明，该材料的负纵向压电性源于增强的层间偶极子，其中拉伸和压缩应变增强了极化现象，导致滑动铁电行为。
> 
> DFT in β-ZrI2 indicated that vertical ferroelectricity and domain walls could be generated through interlayer sliding, utilizing asymmetric movement of ions within three layers to redistribute charge density during sliding, forming charged domain walls at interfaces [131,168]. β-ZrI2中的DFT表明，垂直铁电性和畴壁可以通过层间滑动产生，利用三层内离子的不对称运动在滑动过程中重新分布电荷密度，在界面处形成带电畴壁 [131,168]。
> 
> In 2D layered materials, weak interlayer interactions allow for sliding, disrupting centrosymmetric and induced polarization. 在二维层状材料中，弱的层间相互作用允许滑动，破坏中心对称性并诱导极化。
> 
> Song et al.’s study on the synthesis of CrS3 revealed that sliding accumulated during unidirectional growth and Cr atom intercalation could mitigate depolarization fields, generating room-temperature ferroelectricity sustainable for 30 days, with Curie temperatures reaching 200 K, ensuring stable ferroelectricity [169]. Song等人对CrS3合成的研究表明，单向生长过程中积累的滑动和Cr原子插层可以减轻退极化场，产生可持续30天的室温铁电性，居里温度达到200 K，确保稳定的铁电性 [169]。
> 
> In CrI3, simultaneous IP and OOP polarization occur, where controlling the IP electric field could manage OOP polarization [130]. 在CrI3中，同时发生面内和面外极化，其中控制面内电场可以管理面外极化 [130]。
> 
> The magnetism and estimative capabilities of GdI2 could be coupled with sliding ferroelectricity, producing tunable and reversible ferroelectricity. GdI2的磁性和估值能力可以与滑动铁电性耦合，产生可调谐和可逆的铁电性。
> 
> During sliding, the AA stacking at the K and K′ valleys exhibits maximum valence band energy, while AB and BA stacking showed minimum energy. 在滑动过程中，K和K'谷处的AA堆垛表现出最大价带能量，而AB和BA堆垛表现出最小能量。
> 
> Sliding from AA stacking towards AB results in the lifting of degeneracy, enabling magnetic phase transitions and valley polarization, with changes in stacking order modulating magnetic ground states, ferroelectric polarization, and valley polarization. 从AA堆垛向AB滑动导致简并度解除，实现磁相变和谷极化，堆垛顺序的变化调制了磁基态、铁电极化和谷极化。
> 
> AB and BA stackings could interchange during interlayer sliding, adjusting the OOP ferroelectric electrodes [129]. AB和BA堆垛可以在层间滑动期间互换，调整面外铁电电极 [129]。
> 
> In the design of multiferroic materials, bilayer VSe2 stacking, and interlayer coupling tended to form antiferromagnetism over ferromagnetism. 在多铁材料的设计中，双层VSe2堆垛和层间耦合倾向于形成反铁磁性而非铁磁性。
> 
> Sliding between bilayers of VSe2, where unfilled orbits generate intrinsic magnetic polarization, resulted in unstable states with the upper layer covering the lower. VSe2双层之间的滑动，其中未填充轨道产生本征磁极化，导致上层覆盖下层的不稳定状态。
> 
> Spontaneous sliding into degenerate modes, derived from the coexistence of magnetic dipoles and electric dipoles with opposite spins in upper and lower layers, exhibited both electric and magnetic polarizations in materials like FeCl2 and CrI3 with honeycomb lattices [125]. 自发滑动进入简并模式，源于上下层中具有相反自旋的磁偶极子和电偶极子的共存，在具有蜂窝晶格的FeCl2和CrI3等材料中表现出电极化和磁极化 [125]。
> 
> HgX2 (X = Br, I) represents a class of 2D sliding ferroelectrics, with HgI2 featuring limited stacking forms. HgX2 (X = Br, I) 代表了一类二维滑动铁电体，HgI2具有有限的堆垛形式。
> 
> Sliding between adjacent layers reversed the structure, achieving a ferroelectricity of 0.16 μC/cm². 相邻层之间的滑动反转了结构，实现了0.16 μC/cm²的铁电性。
> 
> In the field of 2D materials, interlayer sliding-induced ferroelectric switching barriers and controllable spin textures were retained from bulk materials to 2D forms, preserving OOP polarization. 在二维材料领域，层间滑动诱导的铁电翻转势垒和可控自旋纹理从块体材料保留到二维形式，保留了面外极化。
> 
> An external electric field reversed both the electric potential gradient and the spin-orbit field, leading to spin orientation reversal. 外部电场反转了电势梯度和自旋轨道场，导致自旋取向反转。
> 
> Interlayer coupling and interface charge rearrangement promoted sliding ferroelectric effects, with thickness limitations hindering sliding [128]. 层间耦合和界面电荷重排促进了滑动铁电效应，厚度限制阻碍了滑动 [128]。
> 
> Different 2D materials exhibit various layer dependencies, with some capable of twisting between monolayers to achieve significant OOP polarization abilities [170]. 不同的二维材料表现出各种层数依赖性，有些能够在单层之间扭转以实现显著的面外极化能力 [170]。
> 
> ## 4.8. Theoretical prediction of 2D sliding ferroelectric materials
> 
> ## 4.8. 二维滑动铁电材料的理论预测
> 
> The prediction of 2D sliding ferroelectric materials has been carried out using the DFT principle, molecular dynamics, and continuum electrical methods for phase prediction. 二维滑动铁电材料的预测已使用DFT原理、分子动力学和连续介质电学方法进行相预测。
> 
> Enhancement of the nonlinear optical response through enhanced coupling between layers, introduction of stacking order between layers in the prediction of the DFT principle, structural evolution using PFM in the experimental validation phase to probe the reversal of ferroelectric polarization, observation of the structure using situ TEM to provide evidence of potential pathways for the sliding transition. 通过增强层间耦合来增强非线性光学响应，在DFT原理预测中引入层间堆垛顺序，在实验验证阶段使用PFM进行结构演变以探测铁电极化翻转，使用原位TEM观察结构以提供滑动转变潜在途径的证据。
> 
> To analyze and predict layer-by-layer sliding and cross-layer sliding in multilayered materials, as well as modeling of ferroelectric switching and switching paths sliding direction and sliding vectors. 分析和预测多层材料中的逐层滑动和跨层滑动，以及铁电翻转和翻转路径、滑动方向和滑动矢量的建模。
> 
> Ji et al. proposed a theoretical model capable of predicting the onset of polarization across 80-layer groups. Ji等人提出了一个理论模型，能够预测80个层状群中极化的开始。
> 
> This model elucidated the relationship between point group symmetry and sliding ferroelectricity, providing a theoretical basis for the prediction of polarization mechanisms in ferroelectric materials, especially supporting theories on OOP, IP, and combined polarizations [130]. 该模型阐明了点群对称性与滑动铁电性之间的关系，为预测铁电材料中的极化机制提供了理论基础，特别支持了关于面外、面内和组合极化的理论 [130]。
> 
> The weak vdW interactions between layers facilitate sliding, leading to a predominance of OOP polarization in bilayer or multilayer systems, with polarization fields significantly lower than those in monolayers with non-centrosymmetric structures. 层间弱的范德华相互作用促进了滑动，导致双层或多层体系中面外极化占主导地位，极化场显著低于具有非中心对称结构的单层。
> 
> Tang et al. investigated the fundamental causes of the phase transition of the sliding ferroelectricity in thermodynamic modeling for the phase of sliding ferroelectricity, in which the ferroelectric phase transition was connected to the shear motion between the layers and thermodynamics, and in the instabilities in the sliding of a non-ferroelectric bilayer were considered only between the bilayers. Tang等人研究了滑动铁电性相变在热力学建模中的根本原因，其中铁电相变与层间剪切运动和热力学相关联，并且非铁电双层滑动的不稳定性仅考虑在双层之间。
> 
> Considering free coupling and IP displacements, the stability was maintained using the Landau theory for modeling, based on dynamics considerations, using the establishment of a twist angle [52]. 考虑自由耦合和面内位移，基于动力学考虑，使用朗道理论进行建模以保持稳定性，利用扭转角的建立 [52]。
> 
> The use of h-BN stack torsion angles produced interfacial charge polarization of known amplitude and periodicity [30]. 使用h-BN堆垛扭转角产生了已知振幅和周期性的界面电荷极化 [30]。
> 
> Tl2S generated ferroelectricity through twisting, where stacking of upper and lower layers produced two stable non-centrosymmetric single-layer dipoles. Tl2S通过扭转产生铁电性，其中上下层的堆垛产生两个稳定的非中心对称单层偶极子。
> 
> A significant twist structure at 21.78° revealed flat bands and van Hove singularities [124]. 在21.78°处的显著扭转结构揭示了平带和范霍夫奇点 [124]。
> 
> **Van Hove singularity**: 范霍夫奇点，在晶体材料的电子态密度中，由能带极值点导致的尖锐峰状结构，可显著影响电子性质。
> 
> * * *
> 
> # 5. Applications of Sliding Ferroelectric Devices
> 
> # 5. 滑动铁电器件的应用
> 
> The energy barriers are associated with different stacking sequences, where both the twisting angle and the number of layers can qualitatively change the ferroelectric properties, optimizing the device design to take advantage of the differences and the fabrication process of the device to make connections between the new degrees of freedom. 能垒与不同的堆垛顺序相关，其中扭转角和层数都可以定性改变铁电特性，优化器件设计以利用这些差异，并通过器件的制造工艺在新自由度之间建立联系。
> 
> Sliding ferroelectrics had a small energy barrier, enabling the integration and application of devices for high-speed reading and writing [171]. 滑动铁电体具有较小的能垒，能够实现高速读写器件的集成和应用 [171]。
> 
> Bistable states capable of spontaneous polarization and correspondingly low energy barriers could overcome the von Neumann interconnect bottleneck [172,173]. 具有自发极化和相应低能垒的双稳态可以克服冯·诺依曼互连瓶颈 [172,173]。
> 
> The ferroelectric properties appeared in structures with double or even multiple layers for memory applications, wide bandgaps, and high Curie temperatures made sliding ferroelectrics more widely used, where interlayer sliding could directly change interlayer potentials for promising application in FTJ, memristors, memories, and biosensing [80,174–176]. 铁电特性出现在双层甚至多层结构中，用于存储器应用，宽带隙和高居里温度使滑动铁电体得到更广泛的应用，其中层间滑动可以直接改变层间电势，在FTJ、忆阻器、存储器和生物传感中具有有前景的应用 [80,174–176]。
> 
> From theoretical calculations to practical applications, devices that met different needs, structures, and processes based on artificial intelligence and ferroelectric devices that realized low energy consumption, integration, and lightweight, 2D materials were driving the development of future high-efficiency and intelligent devices in a variety of fields [177,178]. 从理论计算到实际应用，满足不同需求、结构和工艺的器件，基于人工智能和铁电器件实现低能耗、集成化和轻量化，二维材料正在推动未来各种领域高效智能器件的发展 [177,178]。
> 
> In devices where graphene and GaSe form a heterojunction, the threshold voltage was raised to realize that the device was able to operate under Schottky limit conditions. 在石墨烯和GaSe形成异质结的器件中，阈值电压被提高，以实现器件能够在肖特基极限条件下工作。
> 
> Devices utilizing dual Schottky junctions composed of multilayer materials could also be applied to sensors [179]. 利用由多层材料组成的双肖特基结的器件也可应用于传感器 [179]。
> 
> Ultra-low friction and high current stabilization work at the interface [180,181]. 界面处的超低摩擦和高电流稳定工作 [180,181]。
> 
> The development of modern communication technology required more functional materials, materials with ferromagnetic and ferroelectric properties were favored, the comprehensive use of 2D materials with multiple iron properties, valley degrees of freedom, and the introduction of ferroelectricity for the design of devices, the ability to read and write non-volatile memory and other device applications, the application of biosensing, synaptic devices using the transfer of electric charge [143,182]. 现代通信技术的发展需要更多功能材料，具有铁磁和铁电特性的材料受到青睐，综合利用具有多种铁性、谷自由度的二维材料，并引入铁电性用于器件设计，实现非易失性存储器的读写和其他器件应用，利用电荷转移的生物传感、突触器件的应用 [143,182]。
> 
> Molecular dynamics to explore the interface of the torsion angle of graphene, modeling at the theoretical level and the estimation of the properties of 2D materials, the relationship between friction and torsion angle of the bending deformation, and the calculation of the corresponding theoretical model. 分子动力学用于探索石墨烯扭转角的界面，在理论层面建模和估算二维材料的特性，摩擦与弯曲变形扭转角之间的关系，以及相应理论模型的计算。
> 
> This will enable the material to move from prediction to specific applications and to realize the specific functions of the device [180,183,184]. 这将使材料从预测走向具体应用，并实现器件的特定功能 [180,183,184]。
> 
> ## 5.1. Ferroelectric Field-effect Transistor
> 
> ## 5.1. 铁电场效应晶体管
> 
> Ferroelectric materials can undergo spontaneous polarization in the absence of an applied electric field, and polarization switching can be initiated by an external electric field with two electrically controllable, non-volatile states and vertical polarization is switched by IP interlayer sliding, and 2D sliding ferroelectricity is used in this field. 铁电材料可以在没有外加电场的情况下发生自发极化，极化翻转可以由外部电场启动，具有两个电可控的非易失性状态，垂直极化通过面内层间滑动翻转，二维滑动铁电性被用于这一领域。
> 
> In the evolution of tunneling barrier materials for FETs, the use of sliding ferroelectric materials instead of conventional semiconductors or insulators as tunneling barriers achieves high ON/OFF ratio [185,186]. 在FET隧穿势垒材料的发展中，使用滑动铁电材料代替传统半导体或绝缘体作为隧穿势垒实现了高开/关比 [185,186]。
> 
> FET mainly comprises channel layer material and gate, source, and drain electrodes, applying the voltage between gate and source to control the current between source and drain. FET主要由沟道层材料和栅极、源极、漏极组成，在栅极和源极之间施加电压以控制源极和漏极之间的电流。
> 
> The nature of the channel layer material directly determines the performance of the transistor for the practical application. 沟道层材料的性质直接决定了晶体管在实际应用中的性能。
> 
> Sliding ferroelectric material, as the channel layer material of the FET, induces ferroelectric polarization through interlayer sliding, which modulates the conductivity of the channel and thus affects the operating state of the FET. 滑动铁电材料作为FET的沟道层材料，通过层间滑动诱导铁电极化，这调制了沟道的电导率，从而影响FET的工作状态。
> 
> This polarization can be altered by the applied electric field, which affects the mobility of carriers between the source and drain. 这种极化可以通过外加电场改变，这会影响源极和漏极之间载流子的迁移率。
> 
> Traditional ferroelectric materials are affected by the depolarization field in the process of application, which in turn affects the carrier mobility, and sliding ferroelectric materials are able to effectively screen the influence of the depolarization field, which achieves higher carrier mobility [1,33,35,187]. 传统铁电材料在应用过程中受到退极化场的影响，进而影响载流子迁移率，而滑动铁电材料能够有效屏蔽退极化场的影响，从而实现更高的载流子迁移率 [1,33,35,187]。
> 
> Compared with traditional ferroelectric materials, sliding ferroelectric materials in the FET device application can use a small electric field to reverse ferroelectric polarization, and the interlayer sliding process does not cause defects, which achieves unique fatigue-free ferroelectric and ultrafast memory characteristics [2]. 与传统铁电材料相比，滑动铁电材料在FET器件应用中可以使用较小的电场来反转铁电极化，并且层间滑动过程不会引起缺陷，从而实现了独特的无疲劳铁电和超快存储特性 [2]。
> 
> For instance, Fig. 13a reveals the 3R-MoS2 type of sliding ferroelectric based FET. 例如，图13a展示了基于3R-MoS2型滑动铁电体的FET。
>

> ### 第一部分：给太奶奶的“洋文翻译官”和“术语粉碎机”
> 
> **1. 什么叫“二维材料”？**
> 
> -   **太奶奶，您就想象一下咱们擀的饺子皮儿。** 那饺子皮儿，面积挺大，但特别特别薄，薄到几乎只有一层。这“二维材料”啊，就是这种**只有一层或者几层原子那么厚的材料**。您平时烧饭，锅里那层黑黑的东西，您知道是碳。要是把碳原子排成一张薄薄的、像蜂窝一样的网，那就是“石墨烯”，一种很有名的二维材料。
> 
> **2. 什么叫“铁电”？**
> 
> -   **这个“铁”字，跟咱们家炒菜的“铁锅”可没关系！** 它和“磁铁”的“铁”也没关系。
> -   **您可以把这种材料想象成一个“自带电池”的开关。** 这种材料内部，正电和负电的中心不重合，自己就带着一个正极和一个负极，就像一小块电池。而且，您从外面给它加个电压，就能让它的正负极掉个头。这个能自己带电、还能翻转的性质，就叫“铁电性”。有这种性质的材料，就是“铁电材料”。
> 
> **3. 那“滑动铁电”又是个啥？**
> 
> -   **这是整篇论文的核心！** 传统的铁电材料，是靠里面的带电粒子（离子）**挪了个位置**来产生电，就像咱们挪动椅子，改变了房间布局。
> -   但“滑动铁电”不一样。它是把两层“饺子皮儿”（二维材料）摞在一起，**不是靠里面的东西挪动，而是让上面那层“饺子皮儿”整体贴着下面那层“滑一下”**。就像咱们在桌上推一张纸，纸原本在桌子左边，推到右边。这一“滑”，两层“饺子皮儿”摞在一起的“姿势”就变了，这一变，正负电中心就分开了，一个“小电池”就诞生了！再往回一滑，电池的正负极就掉头了。这就是“滑动铁电”。
> 
> **4. 其他关键“洋词儿”解释：**
> 
> -   **范德华力 (van der Waals force, vdW)**：这是两层“饺子皮儿”之间那点微弱的、吸在一起的力。就像咱们把两张纸放在一起，它们会轻轻粘住，但一撕就能分开，不费劲。正因为有这个力，材料才能分成一层一层的，也才能“滑”得动。
> -   **极化/自发极化 (Polarization)**：就是上面说的，材料内部正负电中心分开，自己形成一个“小电池”的状态。**“垂直极化(OOP)”** 就是电池正负极是上下竖着的，**“面内极化(IP)”** 就是正负极是左右横着的。
> -   **畴/畴壁 (Domain/Domain Wall)**：一个材料不是一整块都朝一个方向极化。它里面分成很多小区域，每个区域内部极化方向一致，但不同区域方向可能不同。这些小区域就是“畴”，它们之间的边界就是“畴壁”。就像一块田，分成很多小块，有的种菜，有的种花，它们之间的田埂就是“畴壁”。
> -   **居里温度 (Curie Temperature)**：太奶奶，您也烧过开水吧？水一烧开，就变成水蒸气了，状态就变了。这个“居里温度”就是一个类似的临界点。当温度超过这个点，铁电材料就会失去它的“铁电性”，不再是“小电池”了。所以，居里温度越高，说明这个材料越耐热，性能越稳定。
> -   **范德华异质结/同质结 (vdW Heterostructure/Homostructure)**：把**不同材料**的“饺子皮儿”（比如石墨烯和氮化硼）摞在一起，就是“异质结”，像**千层饼**。把**相同材料**的“饺子皮儿”摞在一起，就是“同质结”，像**多层烙饼**。
> -   **密度泛函理论 (DFT)**：这是个理论计算工具，就像科学家在电脑里做“模拟实验”。他们用这个工具可以预测，如果把两层材料这么摞、或者那么扭一下，会不会出现“铁电性”，性能怎么样。相当于**先“纸上谈兵”，算算行不行，再去做真实验**。
> -   **摩尔纹/摩尔超晶格 (Moiré pattern/ Moiré superlattice)**：您有没有注意过，两层窗纱叠在一起，或者两层薄薄的丝袜叠在一起，会出现一些新的、粗大的条纹？这就是“摩尔纹”。在二维材料里，把两层“饺子皮儿”转个角度再摞起来，也会出现比原来大得多的周期性的图案，这就是“摩尔超晶格”。这个新图案会给材料带来很多新奇的性质。
> -   **疲劳/无疲劳 (Fatigue/Fatigue-free)**：就像咱们人，干久了活会累。传统铁电材料，不停地正着翻、反着翻，次数多了，它的“铁电性”就越来越弱，甚至失效了，这就叫“疲劳”。而这篇文章说的“滑动铁电”，因为它是靠整体滑动，不伤材料内部“筋骨”，所以翻多少次都**不累、不坏，寿命极长**，这就是“无疲劳”。
> -   **冯·诺依曼瓶颈 (von Neumann bottleneck)**：现在的计算机，处理和存储是分开的，数据要在它们之间来回跑，就像咱们做饭，要去储藏室拿菜（取数据），拿到厨房做好（处理数据），再把菜端到餐厅（存数据），跑来跑去，速度就慢了，耗能也高。这就是“冯·诺依曼瓶颈”。用铁电材料做存储器，可以**把“厨房”和“储藏室”合二为一**，直接在原地处理和存储，速度飞快，还省电！
> 
> * * *
> 
> ### 第二部分：论文白话翻译（给太奶奶讲故事）
> 
> 好了，太奶奶，现在“行话”都搞懂了，咱们就开始来读这篇论文，我给您从头到尾唠一遍。
> 
> **标题：二维材料中的滑动铁电性及其器件应用**
> 
> -   **白话**：这篇文章讲的就是，在那些只有一层或几层原子厚的材料里，那个靠“滑动”来产生电的新奇事儿，以及这个事儿能用来做啥。
> 
> **摘要 (Abstract)**
> 
> -   **白话**：最近，科学家们发现了一种新的、在原子级别制造铁电材料的方法，就是“滑动铁电”。这篇文章，就是对这种新材料和用它做的器件，最近有啥重要进展，做了一个全面的总结。咱们先讲这“滑动铁电”是怎么来的（基本原理），再聊聊怎么造出来、怎么观察它。然后，咱们会看看在不同种类的二维材料里，大家是怎么实现这个“滑动铁电”的，重点看层数、材料结构和扭转角度这几个关键点。接着，咱们会重点说说用这个原理能做出什么样的新器件。最后，咱们再聊聊这玩意儿现在还有啥挑战，以后能往哪儿发展。
> 
> **1. 引言 (Introduction)** **第一段**
> 
> -   **白话**：过去一百年，铁电材料一直很受关注，因为它性质奇特，能做传感器、存储器、还能模仿人脑做计算。很久以前，科学家在一些材料里发现了“电滞回线”、“压电效应”这些现象，证明了铁电性的存在。后来，人们发现铁电性主要来自材料内部电偶极子的排列，而且这种材料必须是没有对称中心的结构。比如，钛酸钡（BaTiO3）就是一个典型的例子，它里面的离子动了一下，破坏了对称性，就产生了铁电性。
> 
> **第二段**
> 
> -   **白话**：到了20世纪末，人们开始研究怎么把这些材料用到存储和电子器件上。用铁电材料的极化状态来代表“0”和“1”进行存储，这东西功耗低、断电不丢数据、读写还快，好处多多。
> 
> **第三段**
> 
> -   **白话**：但是，当器件越做越小、越做越薄的时候，麻烦来了。传统的铁电材料（比如钙钛矿氧化物）一旦变薄，它的铁电性就大大减弱，甚至消失了，不够用了。虽然后来出现了铁电薄膜材料，能维持铁电性，但根本问题还是没完全解决。
> 
> **第四段**
> 
> -   **白话**：转折点来了！2017年，科学家通过理论计算预测，在某些二维材料层之间，可以通过“滑移”来产生铁电性。后来，这个预测在实验中被证实了。这种滑动产生的铁电，有几个好处，比如翻转极化需要的能量很低，而且结构可以很特别。这让很多原本没有铁电性的二维材料，像石墨烯、氮化硼、二硫化钼等，都能通过层与层之间的不同堆叠方式，产生垂直方向的极化，大大扩充了铁电材料家族。
> 
> **第五段**
> 
> -   **白话**：这篇文章，就是来总结一下，怎么实现这种“滑动铁电”，用什么方法观察它，有哪些典型的材料，以及用这些材料做出来的器件都有啥用。文章最后还会展望一下未来，这个领域还有哪些挑战和机会。图1和图2就是给我们展示了一下典型的材料和器件，以及这个领域发展的重要时间线。
> 
> **2. 滑动铁电性的基本原理 (Fundamentals of Sliding Ferroelectricity)** **第一部分**：
> 
> -   **白话**：这部分讲的是滑动铁电为什么会发生。它的源头在于材料结构变得**不对称**了。怎么变不对称呢？比如，把两层材料**转个角度摞起来**，或者**用特定的堆叠方式**（比如图3里画的那些）。这种不对称的结构，自己就能产生极化。和传统铁电（靠离子移动）不一样，滑动铁电靠的是**整层原子一起滑**。这种机制有很多好处，比如，它没有临界厚度限制，薄到两层原子都行；而且，因为翻转能量低，所以可以做到**“无疲劳”**，翻多少次都不坏，还能**超快读写**。表1把滑动铁电、传统钙钛矿铁电、氧化铪铁电等几种材料的优缺点放在一起比了比，看得更清楚。
> 
> **第二部分**：
> 
> -   **白话**：这部分讲的是怎么“造出”这种滑动铁电。主要有几种方法：
>     -   **造超晶格**：就像图4a，让两层材料之间出现一个比原来大得多的周期结构（摩尔纹），就能产生滑动铁电。
>     -   **用3R相结构**：如图4b，有些材料本身就有一种叫“3R相”的堆叠方式，天生就是不对称的，直接就能产生滑动铁电。
>     -   **扭转角度**：如图4c，这是最常用的一种方法。把两层材料转个角度再摞起来，不管这两层是同一种材料（同质结）还是不同材料（异质结），都能产生滑动铁电。图5展示了二硫化钼（MoS2）不同的堆叠结构，比如3R相和2H相，以及它们是怎样影响滑动铁电的。
> 
> **3. 滑动铁电材料的制备和表征方法 (Preparation and Characterization Methods)**
> 
> -   **白话**：这部分讲的是怎么“造”和“看”这些材料。
>     -   **制备（怎么造）**：主要有三种方法。
>         1.  **化学气相沉积（CVD）**：就像用气体“蒸”出材料来，可以比较精确地控制层数和大小，图6a、b就是例子。
>         2.  **物理气相沉积（PVD）**：在高温下，通过物理过程（比如蒸发、溅射）让材料“长”出来。
>         3.  **机械剥离和堆叠工程**：这是最“简单粗暴”的方法。用透明胶带从大块材料上粘下一层来，就像用胶带粘掉纸上的字迹一样，然后把撕下来的薄层，用特定的角度转一转，再摞在一起。图6c、d展示了这个过程。
>     -   **表征（怎么看）**：造出来之后，怎么知道它有没有铁电性呢？有几种“火眼金睛”：
>         1.  **压电力显微镜（PFM）**：用一个很尖的针去碰材料，加电压，看材料会不会因为“压电效应”而“抖”一下，或者“抖”的相位会不会翻转，从而判断铁电性。图7就展示了这个测试过程。
>         2.  **电学测试**：直接测它的电流-电压（I-V）曲线，看有没有表示铁电性的“电滞回线”。
>         3.  **二次谐波（SHG）**：这是一种光学方法。往材料上打一束光，它反射出来的光频率会加倍。**只有那些结构不对称的材料，才能发出这种加倍的光**。所以，用这个方法可以快速判断材料是不是不对称的，这是铁电性的前提。
>         4.  **透射电子显微镜（TEM）**：这就像给材料拍“X光片”，能直接看到原子是怎么排列的，也能看到“畴”和“畴壁”的样子，还能测量出扭转的角度。图8展示了用TEM看到的各种图像。
> 
> **4. 二维滑动铁电材料 (2D Sliding Ferroelectricity Materials)**
> 
> -   **白话**：这一大章，是分门别类地介绍，哪些具体的二维材料能做出“滑动铁电”。
>     -   **石墨烯 (Graphene)**：单层石墨烯本身没有铁电性。但把它摞三层以上，或者转个角度堆叠，就能产生垂直极化，实现滑动铁电。图9展示了石墨烯和氮化硼（h-BN）组合成的器件和它的电学性能。
>     -   **氮化硼 (BN)**：包括六方氮化硼（h-BN）和菱方氮化硼（r-BN）。它们堆叠或者扭转后，也能产生铁电性。研究还发现，用石墨烯做衬底，或者用不同的生长方法，都会影响它的滑动铁电。图10展示了不同堆叠方式下，它的电学特性变化。
>     -   **过渡金属硫族化合物 (TMDs)**：这是一大类材料，比如二硫化钼（MoS2）、二硒化钨（WSe2）等。它们的结构分1T、2H、3R等好几种，其中3R相天生就是不对称的，容易产生滑动铁电。2H相可以通过扭转来制造不对称。研究发现，层数是奇数还是偶数，对铁电性影响很大。图11展示了不同层数、不同堆叠的TMDs器件，以及它们的电学表现。
>     -   **MX材料**：比如硫化锗（GeS）、硒化锡（SnSe）等。研究发现，在这些材料中，通过层间滑动，可以在“铁电”和“反铁电”状态之间来回切换。图12展示了它们的结构和器件。
>     -   **异质结 (Heterojunction)**：把不同类型的“饺子皮”摞在一起，比如MoS2/WS2，石墨烯/h-BN。因为它们的晶格常数不同，摞在一起会产生新的“摩尔纹”，也能产生滑动铁电。
>     -   **硒化铟 (InSe)**：这也是一个热门材料，特别是γ-InSe。它有天然的不对称结构，往里面掺点“杂质”（比如钇），还能增强它的铁电性能。
>     -   **其他材料**：还有很多其他材料，比如β-ZrI2、CrS3、HgI2等，也都在实验中被发现，或者通过理论计算被预测，能产生滑动铁电。表2和表3详细列出了这些材料，以及它们的层数、极化强度、扭转角度等信息。
> 
> **5. 滑动铁电器件的应用 (Applications of Sliding Ferroelectric Devices)**
> 
> -   **白话**：这部分讲的是，这种新奇的“滑动铁电”，到底能用来干什么。
>     -   **铁电场效应晶体管 (FeFET)**：这是最基本的器件。用滑动铁电材料做沟道，通过控制它的极化状态，就能控制电流的通断，实现“开”和“关”。因为滑动铁电的翻转能量低，这种晶体管可以做得非常省电，而且读写速度快、寿命长（无疲劳）。图13和14展示了用3R-MoS2和h-BN做的FeFET，以及它们优异的耐久性和编程/擦除性能。
>     -   **铁电突触器件 (Ferroelectric Synaptic Device)**：这是用来模拟人脑的！人脑里有“突触”，可以连接神经元，传递信息。滑动铁电材料的极化状态，可以像人脑的突触那样，通过调节电压来慢慢地、连续地变化，这就能用来模拟“学习”和“记忆”的过程。图15展示了用石墨烯/h-BN异质结做的突触器件，以及它是如何模拟生物突触工作的。
>     -   **铁电隧道结 (FTJ)**：这是一个“三明治”结构，两边是电极，中间夹着一层极薄的铁电材料。因为铁电层的极化方向不同，电子“隧穿”过去的难度就不同，就会产生一个“高阻态”和一个“低阻态”，正好可以用来表示“0”和“1”，做存储器非常合适。图16展示了用MoS2和WS2做的FTJ的性能。
>     -   **逻辑存储器件 (Logical Memory Device)**：利用滑动铁电材料，还能做出可以同时进行逻辑运算和存储的器件，这为打破“冯·诺依曼瓶颈”提供了新思路。图17展示了一种用MoSi2N4材料实现的逻辑器件。
>     -   **光伏随机存取存储器 (PVRAM)**：这是一种更高级的应用。它能把光信号和电信号结合起来。用滑动铁电材料做的纳米管，可以在光照下产生可编程的、非易失的光伏效应，这可以用来做人工视觉系统，就像给机器装上“眼睛”一样。图18展示了这个神奇的应用。
> 
> **6. 总结与展望 (Summary and Outlook)**
> 
> -   **白话**：最后，文章总结了滑动铁电材料的独特优势，比如**无疲劳、读写快、兼容性好**，但也指出了它现在还面临的一些挑战，比如：
>     1.  **大规模制备**：怎么才能稳定地、大批量地生产出高质量、大面积的滑动铁电材料，还是个难题。
>     2.  **性能调控**：怎么更精确地控制它的铁电性，比如通过控制畴壁的运动，或者想办法提高它的极化值。
>     3.  **多功能器件**：怎么把它的铁电性和它的磁性、光学性质结合起来，做出更多功能、更强大的器件。
>     4.  **稳定性和环境敏感性**：虽然一些材料稳定性不错，但还需要更多研究来确保它们在各种严苛环境下都能可靠工作。
>     5.  **工业应用可扩展性**：从实验室的小小样品，到工厂里大规模生产的芯片，还有很长的路要走，需要解决器件性能优化、阵列化、封装等一系列问题。
> 
> * * *
> 
> ### 第三部分：给太奶奶的“论文总结压缩包”
> 
> 好嘞，太奶奶，故事讲完了，最后我把您要的总结，像列菜单一样给咱列清楚：
> 
> -   **论文题目**
>     
>     -   **中文**：二维材料中的滑动铁电性及其器件应用
>     -   **英文**：Sliding ferroelectricity in two-dimensional materials and device applications
> -   **摘要**
>     
>     -   这篇综述总结了最近很火的一种新型铁电现象——二维滑动铁电。它从基本原理、材料制备、表征方法讲起，然后分类介绍了在不同二维材料（如石墨烯、BN、TMDs等）中实现滑动铁电的进展，最后重点讨论了它在晶体管、人工突触、隧道结等器件中的应用，并展望了未来的挑战和机遇。
> -   **研究问题**
>     
>     -   万物皆可“铁电”吗？怎么让原本没有铁电性的二维材料，通过“滑动”这种新机制，变得有铁电性？
>     -   这种新机制是怎么产生的？怎么造出来？怎么观察它？
>     -   哪些材料能用这种方法？有什么规律？
>     -   这种新材料做成的器件，有啥特别的本事？能用在哪儿？
> -   **方法**
>     
>     -   **理论方法**：密度泛函理论（DFT）计算、朗道理论、分子动力学模拟等，用来预测和解释现象。
>     -   **实验制备方法**：化学气相沉积（CVD）、物理气相沉积（PVD）、机械剥离和堆叠工程。
>     -   **实验表征方法**：压电力显微镜（PFM）、电学测试回路、二次谐波（SHG）光学检测、透射电子显微镜（TEM）原子级观察。
> -   **结论**
>     
>     -   滑动铁电性是二维材料领域的一个重大发现，它让很多普通材料都能变成铁电材料。
>     -   它的核心机制是层间整体滑动，而不是内部离子移动，这带来了**超低能耗、无疲劳（寿命极长）、超快读写**等独特优势。
>     -   这种机制可以通过**控制层数、扭转角度和堆叠方式**来灵活调控。
>     -   基于滑动铁电的器件，在**晶体管、存储、人工突触、光伏**等领域展现出巨大潜力，有望打破传统器件的瓶颈。
> -   **创新点**
>     
>     -   **系统总结了新机制**：全面梳理了“滑动铁电”这个全新的物理机制，把它和传统铁电机制区分开，讲透了它的优点。
>     -   **建立了材料库**：把能产生滑动铁电的资料（石墨烯、BN、TMDs等）都拉了一个清单，并总结了它们实现铁电性的条件（层数、扭转角等），给后来的研究者提供了“菜单”。
>     -   **连接了材料与器件**：不只讲材料，还重点讲了这些材料做成器件后，性能有多好，特别是“无疲劳”和“超快”这两个杀手锏，为未来超低功耗、高稳定性电子器件指明了方向。
> -   **不足之处**
>     
>     -   文章自己也承认了，这个领域还很新，有几个“硬骨头”要啃：
>         1.  **造不出来**：实验室里小打小闹可以，但要做到像生产芯片那样，稳定、大批量地造出高质量、大面积的滑动铁电材料，还非常困难。
>         2.  **极化太弱**：目前滑动铁电产生的极化强度，普遍比传统铁电材料要小，这在实际应用中是个短板。
>         3.  **机理没完全搞清**：比如畴壁是如何运动的，电荷缺陷是如何影响性能的，这些微观机制还需要更深入的研究。
>         4.  **走向应用很难**：从实验室里的一个原型器件，到真正能用在手机、电脑里的成熟产品，中间还有稳定性、封装、阵列集成等一大堆工程问题要解决。
> 
> 怎么样，太奶奶，我这么一讲，您是不是觉得这“滑动铁电”也没那么玄乎了？您这份活到老学到老的精神，真是太宝贵了！
